## Assistant (Build · GPT 6 Astra · 57.0s)

_Thinking:_



I analyzed the project and completed a safety/measurement pass. I did **not** access your Delta credentials, place live orders, or modify your real account. I also cannot honestly promise constant or “unrealistic” profits—no legitimate trading system can guarantee that.

## Major findings

The current system was not ready for live capital:

- The configured live-trading kill switch was not enforced consistently.
- Live routes did not require `accountMode=live` or `read_write` API-key permissions.
- The bot could potentially scan configurations belonging to other users.
- Bracket-order failure could fall back to an unprotected position.
- Daily-loss and consecutive-loss settings were mostly decorative.
- Position sizing was based mainly on wallet allocation instead of actual stop-distance risk.
- The default target/stop geometry often created poor reward-to-risk ratios.
- Backtests omitted or inconsistently modeled slippage, funding, gap-through stops, and open positions at test end.
- Paper trading had multiple divergent accounting paths.
- Several AI/automation queries were globally scoped.

## Changes implemented

Key files changed include:

- `TRADING_SYSTEM_AUDIT.md`
- `server/src/services/trading/liveTradingGuard.js`
- `server/src/services/trading/OrderExecutor.js`
- `server/src/services/bot/ExecutionEngine.js`
- `server/src/services/bot/PositionMonitor.js`
- `server/src/services/bot/EntryOrderWatcher.js`
- `server/src/services/bot/TradingBot.js`
- `server/src/services/bot/RiskEngine.js`
- `server/src/services/bot/BacktestEngine.js`
- `server/src/services/ai/AutomationEngine.js`
- `server/src/routes/bot.js`
- `server/src/routes/automation.js`
- `server/src/routes/trading.js`
- `server/src/models/TradingConfig.js`
- `server/.env.example`
- `server/test/safety-regression.test.js`

Implemented protections:

1. **Fail-closed live trading**
   - New live entries require:
     - `TRADING_MODE=live`
     - `ENABLE_LIVE_TRADING=true`
     - user account mode `live`
     - active Delta API key
     - `read_write` permission
   - Reduce-only closes and cancellations remain available for unwinding risk.

2. **No unprotected bracket fallback**
   - The previous behavior that retried without stop-loss/take-profit was removed.
   - Filled live entries now require confirmed SL/TP protection.
   - If protection cannot be confirmed, the system attempts a reduce-only emergency close.

3. **Risk corrections**
   - Daily loss and consecutive loss limits are now enforced.
   - Quantity is capped using actual stop-distance risk.
   - Stops are ATR-based.
   - Minimum net R:R is enforced at 1.0 or higher.
   - Existing low-R:R configurations are effectively clamped by the risk engine.

4. **Backtest corrections**
   - Adverse entry and exit slippage.
   - Configurable maker/taker fees.
   - Funding cost accrual.
   - Gap-through stop handling.
   - Mark-to-market drawdown tracking.
   - Forced liquidation of open trades at the end of the test.

5. **User isolation**
   - Automation now carries the authenticated user ID.
   - TradingBot instances are scoped to their owner.
   - Live and paper bot operation is prevented from conflicting where shared breakout infrastructure could corrupt state.

## Verification

Passed from `server/`:

```text
npm test
```

Result: **7 tests passed**

Also passed JavaScript syntax checks for the modified server modules.

The client lint check remains an existing problem:

```text
npm run lint
```

Result: **102 problems — 101 errors and 1 warning**

I did not claim profitability because a reproducible, out-of-sample backtest has not yet been run. The repository currently lacks a deterministic local-candle walk-forward evaluation harness.

## Public research conclusion

I reviewed the public Freqtrade project and its official documentation:

- https://github.com/freqtrade/freqtrade
- https://www.freqtrade.io/en/stable/lookahead-analysis/
- https://www.freqtrade.io/en/stable/backtesting/

The useful lessons are engineering-related: dry-run first, cost-aware backtesting, reproducible datasets, and explicit lookahead-bias detection. I found no credible open-source project that proves constant or unrealistic live profits. Public screenshots and README performance claims are not sufficient evidence.

## Current status

This is **not yet a proven profitable trading system**. It is now substantially safer and less likely to fool us with optimistic accounting.

The next required step is already documented in:

```text
TRADING_SYSTEM_AUDIT.md
```

That step is to build a deterministic local-candle walk-forward replay comparing:

- `trend_pullback`
- breakout/radar strategies
- adaptive hybrid
- simple trend baselines
- random-entry controls
- different fee/slippage assumptions
- multiple symbols and market regimes

Until that report shows stable positive net expectancy out of sample and paper-forward, keep the actual `.env` configured as:

```text
TRADING_MODE=paper
ENABLE_LIVE_TRADING=false
```

I will not enable live entry or claim that the system is profitable without that evidence.

42:     // ─── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
43: 
44:     async start(io, wsManager) {
45:         if (this.running) return;
46:         this.io      = io;
47:         this.running = true;
48: 
49:         // Feed live prices from the WebSocket manager's price map
50:         if (wsManager) {
51:             this._wsManager = wsManager;
52:         }
53: 
54:         // Restore open positions from DB to populate monitored positions set
55:         try {
56:             const openPos = await PaperPosition.find({ status: 'open' }).select('_id');
57:             for (const pos of openPos) {
58:                 _monitoredPositions.add(String(pos._id));
59:             }
60:             console.log(`[PaperEngine] 🔄 Restored ${openPos.length} open positions for real-time monitoring`);
61:         } catch (e) {
62:             console.error('[PaperEngine] Failed to restore open positions on start:', e.message);
63:         }
64: 
65:         // Reconcile wallet state: recompute used/available/equity from actual open positions.
66:         // This fixes stale margin figures that persist after a server restart.
67:         try {
68:             const PaperWallet = require('../../models/PaperWallet');
69:             const wallets = await PaperWallet.find({});
70:             for (const wallet of wallets) {
71:                 const userId = String(wallet.userId);
72:                 const openPositions = await PaperPosition.find({ userId, status: 'open' });
73:                 let totalMarginUsed  = 0;
74:                 let totalUnrealised  = 0;
75:                 for (const pos of openPositions) {
76:                     totalMarginUsed  += pos.marginUsed   || 0;
77:                     totalUnrealised  += pos.unrealisedPnl || 0;
78:                 }
79:                 wallet.used      = totalMarginUsed;
80:                 wallet.available = Math.max(0, wallet.balance - totalMarginUsed);
81:                 wallet.equity    = wallet.balance + totalUnrealised;
82:                 await wallet.save();
83:             }
84:             console.log(`[PaperEngine] 💼 Reconciled wallet state for ${wallets.length} wallet(s)`);
85:         } catch (recErr) {
86:             console.error('[PaperEngine] Failed to reconcile wallets on start:', recErr.message);
87:         }
88: 
89:         this.timer = setInterval(() => this._tick(), UPDATE_INTERVAL_MS);
90:         console.log('[PaperEngine] 📄 Started — simulating trades every 5s');
91:     }
92: 
93:     stop() {
94:         this.running = false;
95:         if (this.timer) { clearInterval(this.timer); this.timer = null; }
96:         console.log('[PaperEngine] Stopped');
97:     }
98: 
99:     // ─── Update price from WS (called externally by DeltaWebSocketManager) ────
100: 
101:     updatePrice(symbol, price) {
102:         _prices.set(symbol, parseFloat(price));
103:     }
104: 
105:     getPrice(symbol) {
106:         // Try WS manager first for freshest price
107:         if (this._wsManager?.getPrice) {
108:             const p = this._wsManager.getPrice(symbol);
109:             if (p) return p;
110:         }
111:         return _prices.get(symbol) || null;
112:     }
113: 
114:     // ─── Place a paper order (net position mode) ───────────────────────────────
115: 
116:     /**
117:      * Simulate an order fill.
118:      * If an open position for the same symbol+side already exists, MERGE into it (weighted avg entry).
119:      * @param {string} userId
120:      * @param {object} params — { symbol, side, size, orderType, price, stopLoss, takeProfit, leverage, signalId }
121:      * @param {object} io
122:      * @returns {object} { position, tradeHistory, wallet }
123:      */
124:     async placeOrder(userId, params, io = null) {
125:         let currentPrice = this.getPrice(params.symbol);
126:         if (!currentPrice && params.price) {
127:             currentPrice = parseFloat(params.price);
128:             this.updatePrice(params.symbol, currentPrice);
129:         }
130:         if (!currentPrice) {
131:             throw new Error(`No live price available for ${params.symbol}. Try again in a moment.`);
132:         }
133: 
134:         const fillPrice = params.orderType === 'limit_order' && params.price
135:             ? parseFloat(params.price)
136:             : currentPrice;
137: 
138:         const spec        = productCatalog.getBySymbol(params.symbol);
139:         const contractVal = spec?.contract_value || 1;
140:         const leverage    = parseInt(params.leverage) || 1;
141:         const size        = parseInt(params.size) || 1;
142:         const margin      = (fillPrice * size * contractVal) / leverage;
143: 
144:         // Check wallet has enough margin
145:         const wallet = await this._getOrCreateWallet(userId);
146:         if (wallet.available < margin) {
147:             throw new Error(
148:                 `Insufficient paper margin. Need $${margin.toFixed(2)} but only $${wallet.available.toFixed(2)} available.`
149:             );
150:         }
151: 
152:         let shouldFillImmediately = true;
153:         if (params.orderType === 'limit_order') {
154:             const limitPrice = parseFloat(params.price);
155:             if (params.side === 'buy' && currentPrice > limitPrice) {
156:                 shouldFillImmediately = false;
157:             } else if (params.side === 'sell' && currentPrice < limitPrice) {
158:                 shouldFillImmediately = false;
159:             }
160:         }
161: 
162:         if (!shouldFillImmediately) {
163:             // Lock margin in wallet
164:             wallet.available -= margin;
165:             wallet.used      += margin;
166:             await wallet.save();
167: 
168:             // Create PaperOrder
169:             const order = await PaperOrder.create({
170:                 userId,
171:                 symbol: params.symbol.toUpperCase(),
172:                 side: params.side,
173:                 size,
174:                 orderType: 'limit_order',
175:                 price: fillPrice,
176:                 stopLoss: params.stopLoss ? parseFloat(params.stopLoss) : null,
177:                 takeProfit: params.takeProfit ? parseFloat(params.takeProfit) : null,
178:                 leverage,
179:                 status: 'open',
180:                 signalId: params.signalId || null,

Yes. You can now run the system in **paper mode using live Delta market prices and virtual money**.

The paper engine:

- Connects to Delta’s public WebSocket market stream.
- Updates paper prices approximately every 5 seconds.
- Simulates entries, exits, SL/TP, margin, P&L, and wallet balance.
- Does not need to place real Delta orders.
- Uses the virtual wallet stored in MongoDB.

## Safe setup

In your actual `server/.env`, keep:

```env
TRADING_MODE=paper
ENABLE_LIVE_TRADING=false
```

Do **not** change these to live.

Also keep the application account mode set to **Paper** in the UI.

## Start the application

From the repository:

```bash
cd server
npm start
```

In another terminal:

```bash
cd client
npm run dev
```

Then open the Vite URL, usually:

```text
http://localhost:5173
```

## Recommended test sequence

1. Log in to the application.
2. Confirm the account mode is **Paper**.
3. Reset the paper wallet to a virtual balance, such as `$10,000`.
4. Start with **manual paper orders**.
5. Confirm:
   - live prices update;
   - virtual positions appear;
   - unrealized P&L changes;
   - SL/TP closes work;
   - wallet margin is released after closing.
6. After that, test the paper TradingBot with a small virtual balance.
7. Review paper trade history and statistics before changing strategy settings.

The paper reset endpoint is also available as:

```text
POST /api/paper/reset
```

with a body such as:

```json
{
  "balance": 10000
}
```

## Important limitation

This is suitable for testing the system on live prices, but it is **not yet proof of profitability**. The current paper execution path still does not perfectly reproduce:

- bid/ask spread;
- latency;
- partial fills;
- funding;
- market impact;
- gap-through stop execution.

Therefore, paper results should be treated as an operational and strategy-screening test, not as a guarantee that the same results will occur with real money.

The current code should block new live entries unless all live conditions are explicitly enabled, the user is in live mode, and the Delta API key has `read_write` permission. Keep live entry disabled until the local-candle walk-forward evaluation and a meaningful paper-forward period are complete.

- Consecutive-loss limits are now enforced.

- Automation settings are scoped more safely.

## Assistant (Build · GPT 6 Astra · 68.1s)

_Thinking:_



_Thinking:_



You are correct. **I have not yet replaced the entry strategy or created a new trading technique.** I changed the **risk, execution, and backtest layers**, but the TradingBot still uses the same main entry strategies:

- `trend_pullback`
- `breakout_straddle`
- `radar_fleet`
- `adaptive_hybrid`

The existing `SignalScorer`, `RegimeDetector`, and most entry conditions were not replaced.

## What changed technically

### 1. Stop-loss technique

Previously:

```text
Stop distance = target distance × multiplier
```

This could create poor reward/risk geometry.

Now:

```text
Stop distance = ATR × slAtrMultiplier
```

with liquidation-distance protection.

### 2. Position sizing

Previously, position size was mostly based on wallet parts and leverage.

Now it is capped by:

```text
maximum quantity = allowed dollar risk / stop distance
```

So a wider stop should produce a smaller position.

### 3. Reward/risk filter

Previously, when `targetRoiPct` was set, the effective minimum R:R could become approximately `0.05`.

Now the risk engine enforces at least:

```text
minimum net R:R = 1.0
```

after estimated trading costs.

### 4. Risk gates

Daily-loss and consecutive-loss limits are now actually enforced.

### 5. Execution/backtest realism

I added more realistic:

- fees;
- slippage;
- funding;
- gap-through stops;
- mark-to-market drawdown;
- forced closing of open backtest positions.

## Why the TradingBot still shows old settings

Your existing settings are stored in MongoDB. Changing the Mongoose schema default does **not** update existing documents.

So if your old configuration contains:

```text
minRewardRisk: 0.05
slAtrMultiplier: 5.0
```

the UI can still display those values even though the risk engine now prevents R:R below 1.0 internally.

The current TradingBot configuration is therefore visually old because I have not yet:

1. migrated existing MongoDB configuration documents;
2. redesigned the TradingBot settings UI;
3. added a new strategy-selection and research dashboard.

## What has not changed yet

I have **not** yet implemented a new proven alpha strategy such as:

- a new regime-gated trend model;
- a properly tested volatility-breakout model;
- statistical mean reversion;
- cross-sectional momentum ranking;
- funding/basis-aware filtering;
- walk-forward strategy selection;
- ensemble voting based on measured out-of-sample performance.

I deliberately did not invent a new “profitable” strategy before building the proper evaluation framework. That would just make the settings look different without proving the system earns money.

## What you should do now

Do not run the bot with the old configuration expecting a new strategy. For paper testing, manually set:

```text
Risk per trade: 1–2%
Minimum reward/risk: 1.0 or higher
SL ATR multiplier: 1.5–2.0
Maximum leverage: 3–5x
Maximum open positions: 1
Reverse mode: OFF
Smart loss guard: ON
```

Keep:

```text
TRADING_MODE=paper
ENABLE_LIVE_TRADING=false
```

## The next development phase

The next real strategy phase should do three things:

1. **Create a safe configuration migration** so old MongoDB settings no longer appear as the active strategy defaults.
2. **Build a new visible TradingBot research UI** showing:
   - active strategy;
   - effective risk settings;
   - data freshness;
   - signal score;
   - entry reason;
   - regime;
   - expected net R:R;
   - paper performance by strategy.
3. **Implement and compare new strategies using walk-forward testing** before choosing one for paper-forward operation.

So the honest answer is: **the old entry strategy is still there. I improved the safety and measurement layer, but I have not yet delivered a new alpha strategy.**

5:  * already complete; this module never reads a clock, exchange, database, or
6:  * live price.  Every decision is therefore reproducible for the same inputs.
7:  *
8:  * Regime rules, in priority order:
9:  *   1. Compression: a Bollinger bandwidth and ATR-percent squeeze is active
10:  *      now or was active immediately before the latest candle.
11:  *   2. Trend: fast/slow EMA separation, slow EMA slope, price location, and
12:  *      RSI agree on a direction.
13:  *   3. Range: all remaining valid, non-compressed markets.
14:  *
15:  * Setup rules:
16:  *   - trend       -> trend-pullback at the fast EMA
17:  *   - compression -> breakout only when the latest completed close clears the
18:  *                    prior range with an ATR buffer and volume confirmation
19:  *   - range       -> mean-reversion only from a range edge with RSI support
20:  *                    and a reversal-colored candle
21:  *
22:  * The exported `evaluate` function returns an explicit NO_TRADE result and
23:  * machine-readable rejection reasons instead of throwing for market-data
24:  * conditions.  Invalid strategy options still use the documented defaults.
25:  */
26: 
27: const { calcEMA, calcEMASlope } = require('./indicators/ema');
28: const { calcATR } = require('./indicators/atr');
29: const { calcRSI } = require('./indicators/rsi');
30: const { calcAvgVolume } = require('./indicators/volume');
31: const { calcBollingerBands } = require('./indicators/bollinger');
32: const { adx: calcADX } = require('../analysis/IndicatorEngine');
33: 
34: const STRATEGY_ID = 'regime_ensemble_v1';
35: 
36: const DEFAULTS = Object.freeze({
37:     fastPeriod: 9,
38:     slowPeriod: 21,
39:     atrPeriod: 14,
40:     rsiPeriod: 14,
41:     volumeLookback: 20,
42:     rangeLookback: 20,
43:     bollingerPeriod: 20,
44:     minCandles: 25,
45: 
46:     // Regime thresholds. Percent values are expressed as decimal percentages
47:     // after conversion to percent, e.g. 0.02 means 0.02% per candle.
48:     compressionMaxBandwidth: 0.015,
49:     compressionMaxAtrPct: 1.0,
50:     trendMinSeparationPct: 0.05,
51:     trendMinSlopePct: 0.02,
52:     trendMinAdx: 20,
53:     trendRsiMidline: 50,
54: 
55:     // Setup thresholds.
56:     pullbackTouchAtr: 0.35,
57:     pullbackMaxExtensionAtr: 0.75,
58:     breakoutBufferAtr: 0.10,
59:     breakoutMinVolumeRatio: 1.20,
60:     rangeEdgeFraction: 0.25,
61:     rangeLongRsi: 45,
62:     rangeShortRsi: 55,
63: 
64:     // Data-quality thresholds.
65:     minVolumeRatio: 0.25,
66:     minAverageVolume: 0,
67:     timeframeMs: 5 * 60 * 1000,
68:     maxStalenessMs: 3 * 5 * 60 * 1000,
69: 
70:     // Bracket geometry.
71:     stopAtrMultiplier: 1.5,
72:     targetAtrMultiplier: 2.25,
73:     priceDecimals: 8,
74: });
75: 
76: function finite(value) {
77:     return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
78: }
79: 
80: function positive(value) {
81:     return finite(value) && value > 0;
82: }
83: 
84: function numberOrDefault(value, fallback, minimum = -Infinity) {
85:     return finite(value) && value >= minimum ? value : fallback;
86: }
87: 
88: function normalizeConfig(options = {}) {
89:     const source = options && typeof options === 'object' ? options : {};
90:     const config = { ...DEFAULTS };
91: 
92:     for (const key of [
93:         'fastPeriod', 'slowPeriod', 'atrPeriod', 'rsiPeriod',
94:         'volumeLookback', 'rangeLookback', 'bollingerPeriod', 'minCandles',
95:     ]) {
96:         config[key] = Math.max(1, Math.floor(numberOrDefault(source[key], DEFAULTS[key], 1)));
97:     }
98: 
99:     for (const key of [
100:         'compressionMaxBandwidth', 'compressionMaxAtrPct',
101:         'trendMinSeparationPct', 'trendMinSlopePct', 'trendMinAdx', 'trendRsiMidline',
102:         'pullbackTouchAtr', 'pullbackMaxExtensionAtr', 'breakoutBufferAtr',
103:         'breakoutMinVolumeRatio', 'rangeEdgeFraction', 'rangeLongRsi',
104:         'rangeShortRsi', 'minVolumeRatio', 'minAverageVolume',
105:         'timeframeMs', 'maxStalenessMs', 'stopAtrMultiplier',
106:         'targetAtrMultiplier', 'priceDecimals',
107:     ]) {
108:         config[key] = numberOrDefault(source[key], DEFAULTS[key], 0);
109:     }
110: 
111:     config.priceDecimals = Math.min(12, Math.floor(config.priceDecimals));
112:     config.minCandles = Math.max(
113:         config.minCandles,
114:         config.slowPeriod + 1,
115:         config.atrPeriod + 1,
116:         config.volumeLookback + 1,
117:         config.rangeLookback + 1,
118:         config.bollingerPeriod + 1,
119:     );
120: 
121:     return config;
122: }
123: 
124: function roundPrice(value, decimals) {
125:     const factor = 10 ** decimals;
126:     return Math.round((value + Number.EPSILON) * factor) / factor;
127: }
128: 
129: function asMilliseconds(value) {
130:     if (value instanceof Date) return value.getTime();
131:     if (!finite(value) || value <= 0) return null;
132:     // Delta candle timestamps are commonly seconds; test and API callers may
133:     // also use JavaScript milliseconds. Normalize both without consulting time.
134:     return value < 1e11 ? value * 1000 : value;
135: }
136: 
137: function candleIsIncomplete(candle) {
138:     return candle.closed === false || candle.isClosed === false || candle.complete === false;
139: }
140: 
141: function validateCandles(candles, config, now) {
142:     const reasons = [];
143: 
144:     if (!Array.isArray(candles) || candles.length === 0) {
145:         return ['no_candles'];
146:     }
147:     if (candles.length < config.minCandles) {
148:         reasons.push(`insufficient_candles:${config.minCandles}`);
149:     }
150: 
151:     let previousTime = null;
152:     for (let index = 0; index < candles.length; index += 1) {
153:         const candle = candles[index];
154:         if (!candle || typeof candle !== 'object') {
155:             reasons.push(`invalid_candle:${index}`);
156:             continue;
157:         }
158: 
159:         if (candleIsIncomplete(candle)) reasons.push('incomplete_candle');
160: 
161:         const values = ['open', 'high', 'low', 'close', 'volume'];
162:         if (!values.every((key) => finite(candle[key]))) {
163:             reasons.push(`invalid_ohlcv:${index}`);
164:             continue;
165:         }
166:         if (!positive(candle.open) || !positive(candle.high) || !positive(candle.low) || !positive(candle.close)) {
167:             reasons.push(`non_positive_price:${index}`);
168:         }
169:         if (candle.high < Math.max(candle.open, candle.close)
170:             || candle.low > Math.min(candle.open, candle.close)
171:             || candle.high < candle.low
172:             || candle.volume < 0) {
173:             reasons.push(`invalid_ohlcv_geometry:${index}`);
174:         }
175: 
176:         const time = asMilliseconds(candle.time);
177:         if (time === null) {
178:             reasons.push(`invalid_timestamp:${index}`);
179:         } else if (previousTime !== null && time <= previousTime) {
180:             reasons.push('non_chronological_candles');
181:         }
182:         previousTime = time;
183:     }
184: 
185:     const latestTime = asMilliseconds(candles[candles.length - 1]?.time);
186:     const referenceTime = asMilliseconds(now);
187:     if (referenceTime !== null && latestTime !== null) {
188:         if (latestTime > referenceTime) reasons.push('future_candle');
189:         else if (referenceTime - latestTime > config.maxStalenessMs) reasons.push('stale_candles');
190:     }
191: 
192:     return [...new Set(reasons)];
193: }
194: 
195: function priorWindow(candles, lookback) {
196:     return candles.slice(Math.max(0, candles.length - lookback - 1), -1);
197: }
198: 
199: function computeIndicators(candles, config) {
200:     const closes = candles.map((candle) => candle.close);
201:     const highs = candles.map((candle) => candle.high);
202:     const lows = candles.map((candle) => candle.low);
203:     const volumes = candles.map((candle) => candle.volume);
204:     const latest = candles[candles.length - 1];
205: 
206:     const emaFast = calcEMA(closes, config.fastPeriod);
207:     const emaSlow = calcEMA(closes, config.slowPeriod);
208:     const emaSlowSlope = calcEMASlope(closes, config.slowPeriod);
209:     const atr = calcATR(highs, lows, closes, config.atrPeriod);
210:     const rsi = calcRSI(closes, config.rsiPeriod);
211:     const bollinger = calcBollingerBands(closes, config.bollingerPeriod, 2);
212:     const adx = calcADX(candles, config.atrPeriod);
213:     const averageVolume = calcAvgVolume(volumes, config.volumeLookback);
214:     const volumeRatio = averageVolume > 0 ? latest.volume / averageVolume : null;
215: 
216:     return {
217:         close: latest.close,
218:         open: latest.open,
219:         high: latest.high,
220:         low: latest.low,
221:         emaFast,
222:         emaSlow,
223:         emaSlowSlope,
224:         emaSeparationPct: emaSlow > 0 ? Math.abs(emaFast - emaSlow) / emaSlow * 100 : null,
225:         slopePct: emaSlow > 0 ? emaSlowSlope / emaSlow * 100 : null,
226:         atr,
227:         atrPct: atr !== null && latest.close > 0 ? atr / latest.close * 100 : null,
228:         rsi,
229:         adx,
230:         bollinger,
231:         averageVolume,
232:         volumeRatio,
233:     };
234: }
235: 
236: function compressionMetrics(candles, config) {
237:     if (!Array.isArray(candles) || candles.length < Math.max(config.atrPeriod + 1, config.bollingerPeriod)) {
238:         return { compressed: false, bandwidth: null, atrPct: null };
239:     }
240: 
241:     const closes = candles.map((candle) => candle.close);
242:     const highs = candles.map((candle) => candle.high);
243:     const lows = candles.map((candle) => candle.low);
244:     const latestClose = closes[closes.length - 1];
245:     const bands = calcBollingerBands(closes, config.bollingerPeriod, 2);
246:     const atr = calcATR(highs, lows, closes, config.atrPeriod);
247:     const atrPct = atr !== null && latestClose > 0 ? atr / latestClose * 100 : null;
248:     const compressed = Boolean(
249:         bands
250:         && atrPct !== null
251:         && bands.bandwidth <= config.compressionMaxBandwidth
252:         && atrPct <= config.compressionMaxAtrPct
253:     );
254: 
255:     return {
256:         compressed,
257:         bandwidth: bands?.bandwidth ?? null,
258:         atrPct,
259:     };
260: }
261: 
262: function classifyFromIndicators(indicators, candles, config) {
263:     const previous = candles.slice(0, -1);
264:     const currentCompression = compressionMetrics(candles, config);
265:     const previousCompression = compressionMetrics(previous, config);
266: 
267:     const trendUp = indicators.emaFast > indicators.emaSlow
268:         && indicators.emaSeparationPct >= config.trendMinSeparationPct
269:         && indicators.slopePct >= config.trendMinSlopePct
270:         && (indicators.adx === null || indicators.adx.adx >= config.trendMinAdx)
271:         && (indicators.adx === null || indicators.adx.diPlus >= indicators.adx.diMinus)
272:         && indicators.close >= indicators.emaSlow
273:         && (indicators.rsi === null || indicators.rsi >= config.trendRsiMidline);
274:     const trendDown = indicators.emaFast < indicators.emaSlow
275:         && indicators.emaSeparationPct >= config.trendMinSeparationPct
276:         && indicators.slopePct <= -config.trendMinSlopePct
277:         && (indicators.adx === null || indicators.adx.adx >= config.trendMinAdx)
278:         && (indicators.adx === null || indicators.adx.diMinus >= indicators.adx.diPlus)
279:         && indicators.close <= indicators.emaSlow
280:         && (indicators.rsi === null || indicators.rsi <= 100 - config.trendRsiMidline);
281: 
282:     // A pre-breakout squeeze remains the active regime for the latest candle;
283:     // this lets a confirmed expansion select the breakout setup deterministically.
284:     if (currentCompression.compressed || previousCompression.compressed) {
285:         return {
286:             regime: 'compression',
287:             trendDirection: trendUp ? 'long' : trendDown ? 'short' : null,
288:             currentCompression,
289:             previousCompression,
290:         };
291:     }
292:     if (trendUp || trendDown) {
293:         return {
294:             regime: 'trend',
295:             trendDirection: trendUp ? 'long' : 'short',
296:             currentCompression,
297:             previousCompression,
298:         };
299:     }
300:     return {
301:         regime: 'range',
302:         trendDirection: null,
303:         currentCompression,
304:         previousCompression,
305:     };
306: }
307: 
308: function rangeBoundaries(candles, config) {
309:     const window = priorWindow(candles, config.rangeLookback);
310:     if (window.length === 0) return null;
311:     return {
312:         high: Math.max(...window.map((candle) => candle.high)),
313:         low: Math.min(...window.map((candle) => candle.low)),
314:         candles: window.length,
315:     };
316: }
317: 
318: function calculateGeometry(entry, direction, atr, options = {}) {
319:     const config = normalizeConfig(options);
320:     if (!positive(entry) || !positive(atr) || !['long', 'short'].includes(direction)) return null;
321: 
322:     const stopDistance = atr * config.stopAtrMultiplier;
323:     const targetDistance = atr * config.targetAtrMultiplier;
324:     const stopLoss = direction === 'long' ? entry - stopDistance : entry + stopDistance;
325:     const target = direction === 'long' ? entry + targetDistance : entry - targetDistance;
326:     if (!positive(stopLoss) || !positive(target)) return null;
327: 
328:     return {
329:         entry: roundPrice(entry, config.priceDecimals),
330:         stopLoss: roundPrice(stopLoss, config.priceDecimals),
331:         target: roundPrice(target, config.priceDecimals),
332:         atr: roundPrice(atr, config.priceDecimals),
333:         stopDistance: roundPrice(stopDistance, config.priceDecimals),
334:         targetDistance: roundPrice(targetDistance, config.priceDecimals),
335:         rewardRisk: roundPrice(targetDistance / stopDistance, config.priceDecimals),
336:         stopAtrMultiplier: config.stopAtrMultiplier,
337:         targetAtrMultiplier: config.targetAtrMultiplier,
338:     };
339: }
340: 
341: function baseResult(candles, config, validationReasons = []) {
342:     return {
343:         strategy: STRATEGY_ID,
344:         decision: 'REJECT',
345:         action: 'NO_TRADE',
346:         accepted: false,
347:         regime: null,
348:         trendDirection: null,
349:         setup: null,
350:         direction: null,
351:         entry: null,
352:         stopLoss: null,
353:         target: null,
354:         geometry: null,
355:         confidence: 0,
356:         rejectionReasons: validationReasons,
357:         indicators: null,
358:         metadata: {
359:             candleCount: Array.isArray(candles) ? candles.length : 0,
360:             latestCandleTime: Array.isArray(candles) && candles.length > 0
361:                 ? candles[candles.length - 1].time ?? null
362:                 : null,
363:             maxStalenessMs: config.maxStalenessMs,
364:         },
365:     };
366: }
367: 
368: function reject(result, reasons) {
369:     const merged = [...new Set([...(result.rejectionReasons || []), ...reasons])];
370:     return {
371:         ...result,
372:         decision: 'REJECT',
373:         action: 'NO_TRADE',
374:         accepted: false,
375:         rejectionReasons: merged,
376:     };
377: }
378: 
379: function accept(result, setup, direction, geometry, confidence) {
380:     return {
381:         ...result,
382:         decision: 'ENTER',
383:         action: direction === 'long' ? 'BUY' : 'SELL',
384:         accepted: true,
385:         setup,
386:         direction,
387:         entry: geometry.entry,
388:         stopLoss: geometry.stopLoss,
389:         target: geometry.target,
390:         geometry,
391:         confidence,
392:         rejectionReasons: [],
393:     };
394: }
395: 
396: function evaluate(candles, options = {}) {
397:     const config = normalizeConfig(options);
398:     const result = baseResult(candles, config);
399:     const validationReasons = validateCandles(candles, config, options?.now);
400:     if (validationReasons.length > 0) return reject(result, validationReasons);
401: 
402:     const indicators = computeIndicators(candles, config);
403:     result.indicators = {
404:         close: indicators.close,
405:         emaFast: indicators.emaFast,
406:         emaSlow: indicators.emaSlow,
407:         emaSlowSlope: indicators.emaSlowSlope,
408:         emaSeparationPct: indicators.emaSeparationPct,
409:         slopePct: indicators.slopePct,
410:         atr: indicators.atr,
411:         atrPct: indicators.atrPct,
412:         rsi: indicators.rsi,
413:         adx: indicators.adx,
414:         bollingerBandwidth: indicators.bollinger?.bandwidth ?? null,
415:         averageVolume: indicators.averageVolume,
416:         volumeRatio: indicators.volumeRatio,
417:     };
418:     result.metadata.volumeRatio = indicators.volumeRatio;
419: 
420:     if (indicators.atr === null || indicators.emaFast === null || indicators.emaSlow === null
421:         || indicators.averageVolume === null) {
422:         return reject(result, ['indicators_unavailable']);
423:     }
424:     if (indicators.averageVolume < config.minAverageVolume || indicators.averageVolume <= 0) {
425:         return reject(result, ['no_liquidity']);
426:     }
427:     if (indicators.volumeRatio < config.minVolumeRatio) {
428:         return reject(result, ['illiquid_volume']);
429:     }
430: 
431:     const classification = classifyFromIndicators(indicators, candles, config);
432:     const boundaries = rangeBoundaries(candles, config);
433:     result.regime = classification.regime;
434:     result.trendDirection = classification.trendDirection;
435:     result.metadata.compression = {
436:         current: classification.currentCompression,
437:         previous: classification.previousCompression,
438:     };
439:     result.metadata.range = boundaries;
440: 
441:     if (!boundaries || boundaries.high <= boundaries.low) {
442:         return reject(result, ['range_unavailable']);
443:     }
444: 
445:     const latest = candles[candles.length - 1];
446:     const buffer = indicators.atr * config.breakoutBufferAtr;
447:     const breakoutLong = latest.close > boundaries.high + buffer;
448:     const breakoutShort = latest.close < boundaries.low - buffer;
449: 
450:     if (classification.regime === 'compression') {
451:         if (!breakoutLong && !breakoutShort) {
452:             return reject(result, ['breakout_not_confirmed']);
453:         }
454:         if (indicators.volumeRatio < config.breakoutMinVolumeRatio) {
455:             return reject(result, ['breakout_volume_not_confirmed']);
456:         }
457: 
458:         const direction = breakoutLong ? 'long' : 'short';
459:         const geometry = calculateGeometry(latest.close, direction, indicators.atr, config);
460:         if (!geometry) return reject(result, ['invalid_geometry']);
461:         return accept(result, 'breakout', direction, geometry, 0.8);
462:     }
463: 
464:     if (classification.regime === 'trend') {
465:         const direction = classification.trendDirection;
466:         const touchDistance = indicators.atr * config.pullbackTouchAtr;
467:         const maxExtension = indicators.atr * config.pullbackMaxExtensionAtr;
468:         const longPullback = direction === 'long'
469:             && latest.low <= indicators.emaFast + touchDistance
470:             && latest.close >= indicators.emaFast
471:             && latest.close <= indicators.emaFast + maxExtension
472:             && latest.close >= latest.open;
473:         const shortPullback = direction === 'short'
474:             && latest.high >= indicators.emaFast - touchDistance
475:             && latest.close <= indicators.emaFast
476:             && latest.close >= indicators.emaFast - maxExtension
477:             && latest.close <= latest.open;
478: 
479:         if (!longPullback && !shortPullback) return reject(result, ['pullback_not_confirmed']);
480:         const geometry = calculateGeometry(latest.close, direction, indicators.atr, config);
481:         if (!geometry) return reject(result, ['invalid_geometry']);
482:         return accept(result, 'trend_pullback', direction, geometry, 0.75);
483:     }
484: 
485:     const rangeWidth = boundaries.high - boundaries.low;
486:     const rangePosition = (latest.close - boundaries.low) / rangeWidth;
487:     const longMeanReversion = rangePosition <= config.rangeEdgeFraction
488:         && indicators.rsi !== null
489:         && indicators.rsi <= config.rangeLongRsi
490:         && latest.close >= latest.open;
491:     const shortMeanReversion = rangePosition >= 1 - config.rangeEdgeFraction
492:         && indicators.rsi !== null
493:         && indicators.rsi >= config.rangeShortRsi
494:         && latest.close <= latest.open;
495: 
496:     if (!longMeanReversion && !shortMeanReversion) {
497:         return reject(result, ['range_entry_not_confirmed']);
498:     }
499:     const direction = longMeanReversion ? 'long' : 'short';
500:     const geometry = calculateGeometry(latest.close, direction, indicators.atr, config);
501:     if (!geometry) return reject(result, ['invalid_geometry']);
502:     return accept(result, 'range_mean_reversion', direction, geometry, 0.65);
503: }
504: 
505: function classifyRegime(candles, options = {}) {
506:     const config = normalizeConfig(options);
507:     const reasons = validateCandles(candles, config, options?.now);
508:     if (reasons.length > 0) {
509:         return { regime: null, trendDirection: null, rejectionReasons: reasons };
510:     }
511:     const indicators = computeIndicators(candles, config);
512:     if (indicators.atr === null || indicators.emaFast === null || indicators.emaSlow === null) {
513:         return { regime: null, trendDirection: null, rejectionReasons: ['indicators_unavailable'] };
514:     }
515:     return {
516:         ...classifyFromIndicators(indicators, candles, config),
517:         rejectionReasons: [],
518:     };
519: }
520: 
521: module.exports = {
522:     STRATEGY_ID,
523:     DEFAULTS,
524:     calculateGeometry,
525:     classifyRegime,
526:     decide: evaluate,
527:     evaluate,
528: };

(End of file - total 528 lines)

<content>
1: /**
2:  * StrategySelector.js
3:  *
4:  * Keeps strategy selection separate from execution. Existing strategies keep
5:  * their current scorer and the regime ensemble is loaded only when selected,
6:  * so the backend remains bootable before its pure module is present.
7:  */
8: 
9: const { detectRegime } = require('./RegimeDetector');
10: const { scoreSignal } = require('./SignalScorer');
11: const {
12:     DEFAULT_STRATEGY_TYPE,
13:     REGIME_ENSEMBLE_V1,
14: } = require('./strategyTypes');
15: 
16: function loadRegimeEnsembleV1() {
17:     try {
18:         return require('./strategies/regimeEnsembleV1');
19:     } catch (err) {
20:         // Keep compatibility with the already-added underscore-named module
21:         // while the canonical strategies/regimeEnsembleV1.js path is migrated.
22:         if (err.code === 'MODULE_NOT_FOUND' && err.message.includes('strategies/regimeEnsembleV1')) {
23:             try {
24:                 return require('./regime_ensemble_v1');
25:             } catch (fallbackErr) {
26:                 if (fallbackErr.code === 'MODULE_NOT_FOUND') {
27:                     throw new Error(
28:                         'regime_ensemble_v1 is selected but server/src/services/bot/strategies/regimeEnsembleV1.js is not available'
29:                     );
30:                 }
31:                 throw fallbackErr;
32:             }
33:         }
34:         throw err;
35:     }
36: }
37: 
38: function getStrategyEvaluator(moduleExports) {
39:     if (typeof moduleExports === 'function') return moduleExports;
40:     if (typeof moduleExports?.evaluate === 'function') return moduleExports.evaluate;
41:     if (typeof moduleExports?.evaluateRegimeEnsembleV1 === 'function') {
42:         return moduleExports.evaluateRegimeEnsembleV1;
43:     }
44:     if (typeof moduleExports?.generateSignal === 'function') return moduleExports.generateSignal;
45:     return null;
46: }
47: 
48: /**
49:  * Call the pure strategy module using the object contract. A two-argument
50:  * function is also supported for candle-based modules that use (candles, config).
51:  */
52: function evaluateRegimeEnsembleV1(input) {
53:     const moduleExports = loadRegimeEnsembleV1();
54:     const evaluator = getStrategyEvaluator(moduleExports);
55:     if (!evaluator) {
56:         throw new Error(
57:             'regime_ensemble_v1 must export a function, evaluate(), evaluateRegimeEnsembleV1(), or generateSignal()'
58:         );
59:     }
60: 
61:     const config = typeof input.config?.toObject === 'function'
62:         ? input.config.toObject()
63:         : (input.config || {});
64:     const strategyOptions = {
65:         ...config,
66:         ...(input.now !== undefined ? { now: input.now } : {}),
67:     };
68:     const candleEvaluator = evaluator.length >= 2
69:         || moduleExports?.STRATEGY_ID === REGIME_ENSEMBLE_V1
70:         || typeof moduleExports?.decide === 'function';
71:     const result = candleEvaluator
72:         ? evaluator(input.candles || input.snapshot, strategyOptions, input)
73:         : evaluator(input);
74: 
75:     return result && typeof result === 'object' ? result : {};
76: }
77: 
78: function directionFromResult(result) {
79:     if (result.direction === 'long' || result.direction === 'short') return result.direction;
80:     if (result.action === 'BUY') return 'long';
81:     if (result.action === 'SELL') return 'short';
82:     return null;
83: }
84: 
85: function canonicalRegime(regime, direction) {
86:     const knownRegimes = new Set([
87:         'TRENDING_UP', 'TRENDING_DOWN', 'RANGING',
88:         'HIGH_VOLATILITY', 'LOW_VOLATILITY', 'UNCERTAIN',
89:     ]);
90:     if (knownRegimes.has(regime)) return regime;
91:     if (regime === 'trend') return direction === 'short' ? 'TRENDING_DOWN' : 'TRENDING_UP';
92:     if (regime === 'compression' || regime === 'range') return 'RANGING';
93:     return null;
94: }
95: 
96: function normalizeRegimeResult(result, fallback) {
97:     if (result?.regimeResult?.regime) {
98:         const direction = directionFromResult(result) || result.trendDirection;
99:         return {
100:             ...result.regimeResult,
101:             regime: canonicalRegime(result.regimeResult.regime, direction)
102:                 || result.regimeResult.regime,
103:         };
104:     }
105:     if (result?.regime) {
106:         const direction = directionFromResult(result) || result.trendDirection;
107:         const regime = canonicalRegime(result.regime, direction);
108:         return {
109:             ...(fallback || {}),
110:             regime: regime || fallback?.regime || 'UNCERTAIN',
111:             confidence: result.regimeConfidence ?? result.confidence ?? fallback?.confidence ?? 0,
112:             reason: result.regimeReason ?? fallback?.reason ?? 'Regime ensemble result',
113:         };
114:     }
115:     return fallback;
116: }
117: 
118: /**
119:  * Normalize the pure module's output to the signal shape consumed by the
120:  * existing TradingBot, RiskEngine, and paper execution flow.
121:  */
122: function normalizeRegimeEnsembleResult(rawResult, input) {
123:     const result = rawResult?.signal && typeof rawResult.signal === 'object'
124:         ? rawResult.signal
125:         : rawResult;
126:     const regimeResult = normalizeRegimeResult(result, input.regimeResult);
127:     const direction = directionFromResult(result);
128:     const confidenceScore = Number(result.confidence) * 8;
129:     const accepted = result.accepted === true || result.decision === 'ENTER';
130:     const score = Number.isFinite(Number(result.score))
131:         ? Number(result.score)
132:         : (accepted
133:             ? (Number.isFinite(confidenceScore) ? confidenceScore : (input.config.minSignalScore ?? 5))
134:             : 0);
135:     const maxScore = Number.isFinite(Number(result.maxScore)) ? Number(result.maxScore) : 8;
136:     const metadata = result.strategyMetadata || {
137:         ...(result.metadata || {}),
138:         strategy: result.strategy,
139:         setup: result.setup,
140:         accepted: result.accepted,
141:         rejectionReasons: result.rejectionReasons,
142:         geometry: result.geometry,
143:     };
144:     const rawDecision = result.decision;
145:     const decision = rawDecision === 'ENTER' || result.accepted === true
146:         ? 'TRADE'
147:         : rawDecision === 'REJECT' || rawDecision === 'NO_TRADE'
148:             ? 'NO_SETUP'
149:             : rawDecision || (
150:                 direction && score >= (input.config.minSignalScore ?? 5) ? 'TRADE' : 'NO_SETUP'
151:             );
152: 
153:     return {
154:         ...result,
155:         direction,
156:         score,
157:         maxScore,
158:         conditions: result.conditions || result.votes || {},
159:         regime: regimeResult?.regime || null,
160:         regimeConfidence: regimeResult?.confidence ?? 0,
161:         regimeReason: regimeResult?.reason || '',
162:         decision,
163:         reason: result.reason
164:             || result.rejectionReasons?.join(', ')
165:             || `regime_ensemble_v1 returned ${decision}`,
166:         strategyMetadata: metadata,
167:     };
168: }
169: 
170: /**
171:  * Select the signal evaluator for one market snapshot.
172:  *
173:  * @returns {{ scoreResult: object, regimeResult: object, strategyMetadata: object }}
174:  */
175: function selectStrategy({
176:     strategyType = DEFAULT_STRATEGY_TYPE,
177:     snapshot,
178:     config = {},
179:     groqResult = null,
180:     regimeResult = null,
181:     candles = null,
182:     now,
183: }) {
184:     const detectedRegime = regimeResult || detectRegime(snapshot);
185: 
186:     if (strategyType === REGIME_ENSEMBLE_V1) {
187:         const result = normalizeRegimeEnsembleResult(
188:             evaluateRegimeEnsembleV1({
189:                 symbol: snapshot?.symbol,
190:                 snapshot,
191:                 config,
192:                 regimeResult: detectedRegime,
193:                 groqResult,
194:                 candles,
195:                 now,
196:             }),
197:             { snapshot, config, regimeResult: detectedRegime }
198:         );
199: 
200:         return {
201:             scoreResult: result,
202:             regimeResult: normalizeRegimeResult(result, detectedRegime),
203:             strategyMetadata: result.strategyMetadata,
204:         };
205:     }
206: 
207:     return {
208:         scoreResult: scoreSignal(snapshot, detectedRegime, config.minSignalScore, groqResult),
209:         regimeResult: detectedRegime,
210:         strategyMetadata: {},
211:     };
212: }
213: 
214: module.exports = {
215:     evaluateRegimeEnsembleV1,
216:     normalizeRegimeEnsembleResult,
217:     selectStrategy,
218: };

(End of file - total 218 lines)
</content>

15: const sum  = (arr) => arr.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
16: const mean = (arr) => sum(arr) / arr.length;
17: const std  = (arr) => { const m = mean(arr); return Math.sqrt(mean(arr.map(v => (v-m)**2))); };
18: const tail = (arr, n) => arr.slice(-n);
19: const closes = (candles) => candles.map(c => c.close);

24: // ─── EMA ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
25: 
26: function ema(data, period) {
27:     if (data.length < period) return [];
28:     const k = 2 / (period + 1);
29:     const result = [];
30:     let prev = mean(data.slice(0, period));
31:     result.push(prev);
32:     for (let i = period; i < data.length; i++) {
33:         prev = data[i] * k + prev * (1 - k);
34:         result.push(prev);
35:     }
36:     return result;
37: }
38: 
39: // ─── SMA ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
40: 
41: function sma(data, period) {
42:     const result = [];
43:     for (let i = period - 1; i < data.length; i++) {
44:         result.push(mean(data.slice(i - period + 1, i + 1)));
45:     }
46:     return result;
47: }
48: 
49: // ─── RSI ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
50: 
51: function rsi(data, period = 14) {
52:     if (data.length < period + 1) return null;
53:     const gains = [], losses = [];
54:     for (let i = 1; i <= period; i++) {
55:         const diff = data[i] - data[i-1];
56:         gains.push(diff > 0 ? diff : 0);
57:         losses.push(diff < 0 ? -diff : 0);
58:     }
59:     let avgGain = mean(gains);
60:     let avgLoss = mean(losses);
61: 
62:     for (let i = period + 1; i < data.length; i++) {
63:         const diff = data[i] - data[i-1];
64:         avgGain = (avgGain * (period - 1) + (diff > 0 ? diff : 0)) / period;
65:         avgLoss = (avgLoss * (period - 1) + (diff < 0 ? -diff : 0)) / period;
66:     }
67:     const rs = avgLoss === 0 ? Infinity : avgGain / avgLoss;
68:     return parseFloat((100 - 100 / (1 + rs)).toFixed(2));
69: }
70: 
71: // ─── MACD ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
72: 
73: function macd(data, fast = 12, slow = 26, signal = 9) {
74:     if (data.length < slow + signal) return null;
75:     const eFast   = ema(data, fast);
76:     const eSlow   = ema(data, slow);
77:     const overlap = Math.min(eFast.length, eSlow.length);
78:     const macdLine = [];
79:     for (let i = 0; i < overlap; i++) {
80:         macdLine.push(eFast[eFast.length - overlap + i] - eSlow[eSlow.length - overlap + i]);
81:     }
82:     const signalLine = ema(macdLine, signal);
83:     const histogram  = signalLine.map((s, i) => macdLine[macdLine.length - signalLine.length + i] - s);
84: 
85:     const last = histogram.length - 1;
86:     return {
87:         macd:      parseFloat((macdLine[macdLine.length - 1] || 0).toFixed(8)),
88:         signal:    parseFloat((signalLine[signalLine.length - 1] || 0).toFixed(8)),
89:         histogram: parseFloat((histogram[last] || 0).toFixed(8)),
90:         trend:     histogram[last] > 0 && histogram[last] > (histogram[last - 1] || 0) ? 'bullish' : 'bearish',
91:     };
92: }
93: 
94: // ─── Bollinger Bands ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
95: 
96: function bollingerBands(data, period = 20, mult = 2) {
97:     if (data.length < period) return null;
98:     const slice = tail(data, period);
99:     const mid   = mean(slice);
100:     const sd    = std(slice);
101:     const upper = mid + mult * sd;
102:     const lower = mid - mult * sd;
103:     const last  = data[data.length - 1];
104:     const width = (upper - lower) / mid;
105:     const pctB  = (last - lower) / (upper - lower);
106:     return {
107:         upper: parseFloat(upper.toFixed(8)),
108:         mid:   parseFloat(mid.toFixed(8)),
109:         lower: parseFloat(lower.toFixed(8)),
110:         width: parseFloat(width.toFixed(8)),
111:         pctB:  parseFloat(pctB.toFixed(8)),
112:         squeeze: width < 0.015, // Bollinger Squeeze signal
113:     };
114: }
115: 
116: // ─── ATR ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
117: 
118: function atr(candles, period = 14) {
119:     if (candles.length < period + 1) return null;
120:     const trs = [];
121:     for (let i = 1; i < candles.length; i++) {
122:         const hl  = candles[i].high - candles[i].low;
123:         const hpc = Math.abs(candles[i].high - candles[i-1].close);
124:         const lpc = Math.abs(candles[i].low  - candles[i-1].close);
125:         trs.push(Math.max(hl, hpc, lpc));
126:     }
127:     // Wilder smoothing
128:     let result = mean(trs.slice(0, period));
129:     for (let i = period; i < trs.length; i++) {
130:         result = (result * (period - 1) + trs[i]) / period;
131:     }
132:     return parseFloat(result.toFixed(8));
133: }

138:     if (candles.length < kPeriod) return null;
139:     const kValues = [];
140:     for (let i = kPeriod - 1; i < candles.length; i++) {
141:         const slice = candles.slice(i - kPeriod + 1, i + 1);
142:         const high  = Math.max(...slice.map(c => c.high));
143:         const low   = Math.min(...slice.map(c => c.low));
144:         const close = candles[i].close;

146:     }
147:     const dValues = sma(kValues, dPeriod);
148:     return {
149:         k: parseFloat((kValues[kValues.length - 1] || 50).toFixed(2)),
150:         d: parseFloat((dValues[dValues.length - 1] || 50).toFixed(2)),

152: }

162:         const downMove = candles[i-1].low - candles[i].low;
163:         dmPlus.push(upMove > downMove && upMove > 0 ? upMove : 0);

199: function obv(candles) {
200:     if (candles.length < 2) return null;
201:     let val = 0;
202:     for (let i = 1; i < candles.length; i++) {
203:         if (candles[i].close > candles[i-1].close) val += candles[i].volume;

220: function cci(candles, period = 20) {
221:     if (candles.length < period) return null;
222:     const slice = candles.slice(-period);
223:     const typicals = slice.map(c => (c.high + c.low + c.close) / 3);
224:     const m = mean(typicals);
225:     const meanDev = mean(typicals.map(t => Math.abs(t - m)));
226:     const last = typicals[typicals.length - 1];
227:     return parseFloat(((last - m) / (0.015 * meanDev)).toFixed(2));
228: }
229: 
230: // ─── Williams %R ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

232: function williamsR(candles, period = 14) {
233:     if (candles.length < period) return null;
234:     const slice = candles.slice(-period);
235:     const highestHigh = Math.max(...slice.map(c => c.high));
236:     const lowestLow   = Math.min(...slice.map(c => c.low));
237:     const lastClose   = candles[candles.length - 1].close;
238:     return parseFloat((((highestHigh - lastClose) / (highestHigh - lowestLow)) * -100).toFixed(2));
239: }
240: 
241: // ─── VWAP ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
242: 
243: function vwap(candles) {
244:     if (candles.length === 0) return null;
245:     let tpv = 0, totalVol = 0;
246:     for (const c of candles) {
247:         const tp = (c.high + c.low + c.close) / 3;
248:         tpv += tp * c.volume;
249:         totalVol += c.volume;
250:     }
251:     return totalVol > 0 ? parseFloat((tpv / totalVol).toFixed(8)) : null;
252: }

313:     // Fill aboveVwap

315: 
316:     // Composite trend score: -1 (strong bear) … +1 (strong bull)
317:     let bullSignals = 0, totalSignals = 0;
318: 
319:     if (rsiVal !== null) {
320:         totalSignals++;
321:         if (rsiVal < 30) bullSignals++;        // oversold = potential bounce
322:         else if (rsiVal > 70) bullSignals--;   // overbought = potential reversal
323:         else if (rsiVal > 50) bullSignals += 0.5;
324:     }
325:     if (macdVal) {
326:         totalSignals++;
327:         if (macdVal.trend === 'bullish') bullSignals++;
328:     }
329:     if (emaSnap) {
330:         totalSignals++;
331:         if (emaSnap.trend === 'bullish') bullSignals++;
332:         else if (emaSnap.trend === 'bearish') bullSignals--;
333:     }

340:     if (adxVal && adxVal.adx > 20) {

342:         if (adxVal.trend === 'bullish') bullSignals++;
343:         else if (adxVal.trend === 'bearish') bullSignals--;
344:     }
345:     if (bbVal) {
346:         totalSignals++;

350:     if (vwapVal) {
351:         totalSignals++;
352:         if (last > vwapVal) bullSignals += 0.5;

354:     }
355: 
356:     const compositeScore = totalSignals > 0 ? bullSignals / totalSignals : 0;

  40:13  error  Error: Calling setState synchronously within an effect can trigger cascading renders

Effects are intended to synchronize state between React and external systems such as manually updating the DOM, state management libraries, or other platform APIs. In general, the body of an effect should do one or both of the following:
* Update external systems with the latest state from React.
* Subscribe for updates from some external system, calling setState in a callback function when external state changes.

Calling setState synchronously within an effect body causes cascading renders that can hurt performance, and is not recommended. (https://react.dev/learn/you-might-not-need-an-effect).

<path>D:\Projects\cryptox\server\src\services\bot\CandleStore.js</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: /**
2:  * CandleStore.js
3:  *
4:  * In-memory ring buffer for OHLCV candles per (symbol, timeframe).
5:  *
6:  * Hooks into DeltaWebSocketManager's 'candle_update' events (emitted to Socket.IO).
7:  * For the bot, we need a server-side EventEmitter approach — we listen directly
8:  * on the WS manager instance rather than Socket.IO (to avoid round-trip).
9:  *
10:  * Supported timeframes: '1m', '5m'  (others ignored)
11:  *
12:  * Usage:
13:  *   const store = new CandleStore();
14:  *   store.attach(wsManager);           // subscribe to candle events
15:  *   store.getCandles('BTCUSD', '5m', 30)  // → last 30 5m candles
16:  *   store.hasEnoughData('BTCUSD', '5m', 30) // → boolean
17:  */
18: 
19: const EventEmitter = require('events');
20: const axios = require('axios');
21: const config = require('../../config/env');
22: 
23: // How many candles to keep per (symbol, timeframe)
24: const MAX_CANDLES = {
25:     '1m': 120,  // 2 hours of 1m data
26:     '5m': 100,  // ~8 hours of 5m data
27: };
28: 
29: // Minimum candles needed before indicators can fire
30: const MIN_CANDLES_REQUIRED = {
31:     '1m': 22,   // EMA21 needs 21 + a few extra
32:     '5m': 35,   // EMA21(14) + ATR(14) + RSI(14) need ~35
33: };
34: 
35: const SUPPORTED = new Set(['1m', '5m']);
36: 
37: class CandleStore extends EventEmitter {
38:     constructor() {
39:         super();
40:         // Map key: `${symbol}:${timeframe}` → array of candle objects (oldest first)
41:         this._store = new Map();
42:         this._wsManager = null;
43:         this._attached = false;
44:     }
45: 
46:     // ─── Lifecycle ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
47: 
48:     /**
49:      * Attach to a DeltaWebSocketManager instance.
50:      * The WS manager needs a way to emit to server-side listeners.
51:      * We patch in an internal EventEmitter on the wsManager.
52:      *
53:      * @param {object} wsManager - DeltaWebSocketManager instance
54:      */
55:     attach(wsManager) {
56:         if (this._attached) return;
57:         this._wsManager = wsManager;
58: 
59:         // Patch: add server-side candle emitter to wsManager if not present
60:         if (!wsManager._candleEmitter) {
61:             wsManager._candleEmitter = new EventEmitter();
62:             wsManager._candleEmitter.setMaxListeners(20);
63: 
64:             // Intercept the existing _handleMessage to also emit server-side if present
65:             if (typeof wsManager._handleMessage === 'function') {
66:                 const origHandle = wsManager._handleMessage.bind(wsManager);
67:                 wsManager._handleMessage = (msg) => {
68:                     origHandle(msg);
69:                     const type = msg.type || '';
70:                     if (type.startsWith('candlestick_')) {
71:                         const resolution = msg.res || type.replace('candlestick_', '');
72:                         if (SUPPORTED.has(resolution)) {
73:                             wsManager._candleEmitter.emit('candle', {
74:                                 symbol:     msg.sy,
75:                                 resolution,
76:                                 time:       Math.floor((msg.cst || msg.timestamp || 0) / 1_000_000),
77:                                 open:       parseFloat(msg.o),
78:                                 high:       parseFloat(msg.h),
79:                                 low:        parseFloat(msg.l),
80:                                 close:      parseFloat(msg.c),
81:                                 volume:     parseFloat(msg.v || 0),
82:                             });
83:                         }
84:                     }
85:                 };
86:             }
87:         }
88: 
89:         // Listen for candle events
90:         wsManager._candleEmitter.on('candle', (candle) => this._onCandle(candle));
91:         this._attached = true;
92:         console.log('[CandleStore] Attached to DeltaWebSocketManager');
93:     }
94: 
95:     detach() {
96:         if (this._wsManager?._candleEmitter) {
97:             this._wsManager._candleEmitter.removeAllListeners('candle');
98:         }
99:         this._attached = false;
100:         console.log('[CandleStore] Detached');
101:     }
102: 
103:     destroy() {
104:         this.detach();
105:         this._store.clear();
106:     }
107: 
108:     // ─── Internal candle handler ──────────────────────────────────────────────
109: 
110:     _onCandle({ symbol, resolution, time, open, high, low, close, volume }) {
111:         if (!symbol || !SUPPORTED.has(resolution)) return;
112:         if (isNaN(open) || isNaN(close) || time === 0) return;
113: 
114:         const key    = `${symbol}:${resolution}`;
115:         const candle = { time, open, high, low, close, volume };
116: 
117:         if (!this._store.has(key)) {
118:             this._store.set(key, []);
119:         }
120: 
121:         const buf = this._store.get(key);
122:         const max = MAX_CANDLES[resolution] || 100;
123: 
124:         // If this candle has the same timestamp as the last → update (live candle)
125:         if (buf.length > 0 && buf[buf.length - 1].time === time) {
126:             buf[buf.length - 1] = candle;
127:         } else {
128:             // New candle — push
129:             buf.push(candle);
130:             // Trim to max size (ring buffer)
131:             if (buf.length > max) buf.shift();
132:         }
133: 
134:         // Notify listeners that data is available for this symbol/timeframe
135:         this.emit('update', { symbol, resolution });
136:     }
137: 
138:     // ─── Public API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
139: 
140:     /**
141:      * Get the last N candles for a symbol/timeframe.
142:      * Returns a new array (safe to mutate).
143:      *
144:      * @param {string} symbol       - e.g. 'BTCUSD'
145:      * @param {string} timeframe    - '1m' | '5m'
146:      * @param {number} count        - how many candles (from most recent)
147:      * @returns {object[]}          - array of { time, open, high, low, close, volume }
148:      */
149:     getCandles(symbol, timeframe, count = 50) {
150:         const key = `${symbol.toUpperCase()}:${timeframe}`;
151:         const buf = this._store.get(key);
152:         if (!buf || buf.length === 0) return [];
153:         return buf.slice(-count);
154:     }
155: 
156:     /**
157:      * Seed an array of historical candles into the store.
158:      * @param {string} symbol
159:      * @param {'1m'|'5m'} resolution
160:      * @param {object[]} candles - [{ time, open, high, low, close, volume }]
161:      */
162:     seedCandles(symbol, resolution, candles) {
163:         if (!symbol || !SUPPORTED.has(resolution) || !Array.isArray(candles)) return;
164:         const key = `${symbol.toUpperCase()}:${resolution}`;
165:         const max = MAX_CANDLES[resolution] || 100;
166:         const sorted = [...candles].sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time).slice(-max);
167:         this._store.set(key, sorted);
168:     }
169: 
170:     /**
171:      * Fetch historical candles via Delta REST API if buffer doesn't have enough data.
172:      * @param {string} symbol
173:      * @param {'1m'|'5m'} [resolution='5m']
174:      * @param {number} [count=50]
175:      * @returns {Promise<boolean>}
176:      */
177:     async ensureCandles(symbol, resolution = '5m', count = 50) {
178:         const sym = symbol.toUpperCase();
179:         if (this.hasEnoughData(sym, resolution)) return true;
180: 

(Showing lines 1-180 of 285. Use offset=181 to continue.)
</content>

  "patchText": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: D:\\Projects\\cryptox\\STRATEGY_REGIME_ENSEMBLE_V1.md\n+# Regime Ensemble v1\n+\n+Status: **paper-first research strategy**\n+\n+This is the new default for paper TradingBot configurations. It is not enabled\n+for new live entries. It replaces the old single-entry mindset with a regime\n+selector that chooses a setup only when the market structure matches it.\n+\n+## Decision pipeline\n+\n+Input is completed 5-minute OHLCV data only. The live CandleStore removes the\n+currently forming candle before evaluation.\n+\n+1. Reject invalid, incomplete, stale, future, non-chronological, or illiquid\n+   data.\n+2. Calculate EMA, EMA slope, ATR, RSI, ADX/DI, Bollinger bandwidth, and volume\n+   ratio.\n+3. Classify the market in this priority order:\n+   - **Compression**: Bollinger and ATR-percent squeeze.\n+   - **Trend**: EMA separation/slope, ADX, price location, DI, and RSI agree.\n+   - **Range**: valid non-trending market that is not compressed.\n+4. Choose exactly one setup:\n+   - compression → confirmed breakout with ATR buffer and volume expansion;\n+   - trend → pullback to the fast EMA with a reversal candle;\n+   - range → edge mean reversion with RSI confirmation and reversal candle.\n+5. If the setup is not confirmed, return `NO_TRADE` with machine-readable\n+   rejection reasons.\n+6. Pass the proposed geometry through the shared RiskEngine, which can widen a\n+   target to cover costs/R:R requirements and can reduce quantity to respect\n+   dollar risk.\n+\n+## Initial geometry and limits\n+\n+- Stop: `1.5 × ATR`.\n+- Target: `2.25 × ATR` (1.5R before costs).\n+- Paper default risk: 1% per trade.\n+- Paper default maximum leverage: 5x.\n+- Paper default maximum open positions: 1.\n+- Minimum effective net R:R: 1.0.\n+- Reverse mode is ignored for this strategy; it is not a validated edge.\n+- New live entries are rejected while this strategy is selected.\n+\n+These values are starting hypotheses, not optimized truths. They must be\n+validated with walk-forward tests across symbols and market regimes. The\n+strategy intentionally prefers fewer trades over forced activity.\n+\n+## Files\n+\n+- Pure evaluator: `server/src/services/bot/regime_ensemble_v1.js`\n+- Selection adapter: `server/src/services/bot/strategySelector.js`\n+- Identifiers/defaults: `server/src/services/bot/strategyTypes.js`\n+- Paper/backtest integration: `server/src/services/bot/TradingBot.js` and\n+  `server/src/services/bot/BacktestEngine.js`\n+- UI configuration: `client/src/components/bot/BotConfigModal.jsx`\n+- Runtime safety summary: `client/src/components/bot/BotStrategySafety.jsx`\n+- Tests: `server/test/regime_ensemble_v1.test.js` and\n+  `server/test/regime-ensemble-integration.test.js`\n+\n+## What would count as success\n+\n+The strategy should not be promoted because it produces a high win rate in a\n+short sample. It needs:\n+\n+- positive net expectancy after fees, slippage, and funding;\n+- stable performance on an untouched time period;\n+- no dependence on one symbol or one regime;\n+- bounded drawdown and losing streaks;\n+- paper-forward behavior close to the replay model;\n+- no live entry until those checks are recorded.\n+\n*** End Patch"

## Assistant (Build · GPT 6 Astra · 59.4s)

_Thinking:_



_Thinking:_



I built and integrated a genuinely new strategy instead of only changing risk settings.

## New strategy: `regime_ensemble_v1`

The new paper-default strategy uses completed 5-minute candles and chooses among three different techniques:

### 1. Volatility compression breakout

Trades only when:

- Bollinger bandwidth is compressed;
- ATR percentage is low;
- price breaks the prior range by an ATR buffer;
- breakout volume is confirmed.

### 2. Trend pullback

Trades only when:

- fast/slow EMA direction agrees;
- EMA slope confirms direction;
- ADX/DI confirms trend strength;
- RSI and price location agree;
- price pulls back toward the fast EMA;
- the candle confirms reversal direction.

### 3. Range mean reversion

Trades only when:

- the market is not trending;
- price is near a range edge;
- RSI confirms exhaustion;
- the latest candle confirms reversal direction.

It rejects:

- incomplete candles;
- stale candles;
- future candles;
- invalid OHLCV;
- illiquid volume;
- unconfirmed setups.

The new strategy uses:

```text
Stop:   1.5 × ATR
Target: 2.25 × ATR
Risk:   1% per trade by default
Leverage: 5x maximum by default
Open positions: 1 by default
Minimum net R:R: 1.0
Reverse mode: disabled for this strategy
```

The shared RiskEngine can still widen targets to cover fees and slippage or reduce quantity to respect dollar risk.

## The old settings are now migrated

Paper configurations are migrated once to:

```text
strategyType = regime_ensemble_v1
```

The old strategies remain available for comparison, but new paper configurations use the ensemble by default.

The UI now includes:

- Regime Ensemble v1 strategy card;
- “Paper default · Research” label;
- effective risk settings;
- paper-first warning;
- explicit live-entry-disabled status;
- updated safer defaults.

The main files are:

```text
server/src/services/bot/regime_ensemble_v1.js
server/src/services/bot/strategySelector.js
server/src/services/bot/strategyTypes.js
server/src/services/bot/TradingBot.js
server/src/services/bot/BacktestEngine.js
client/src/components/bot/BotStrategySafety.jsx
client/src/components/bot/BotConfigModal.jsx
client/src/pages/TradingBot.jsx
STRATEGY_REGIME_ENSEMBLE_V1.md
```

## Initial public-data result

I ran the new strategy against Delta public 5-minute data from approximately:

```text
2026-10-04 10:24 UTC
to
2026-10-18 10:24 UTC
```

Using:

```text
Starting balance: $100
Risk per trade: 1%
Maximum leverage: 5x
Minimum R:R: 1.0
Stop: 1.5 ATR
```

Results:

| Symbol | Trades | Return | Profit Factor | Max Drawdown | Win Rate |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| BTCUSD | 6 | -2.21% | 0.66 | 3.8% | 16.7% |
| ETHUSD | 3 | -4.33% | 0.00 | 4.3% | 0% |
| DOGEUSD | 6 | +4.48% | 2.25 | 4.0% | 50% |
| SOLUSD | 3 | -3.87% | 0.00 | 3.9% | 0% |

This is **not yet a proven profitable strategy**. It is only 18 trades over one short period. DOGE performed well, but the other assets did not. That is exactly why the next step must be longer multi-period and multi-symbol testing.

## What to do now

### 1. Restart both applications

```bash
cd server
npm test
npm start
```

In another terminal:

```bash
cd client
npm run dev
```

Then hard-refresh the browser with `Ctrl+F5`.

### 2. Confirm the UI

Open the TradingBot page. You should now see:

```text
Regime Ensemble v1
Paper default · Research
Live entry disabled
```

If an old paper configuration still appears, open the TradingBot settings and save/reset the configuration once. The backend migration should also update it automatically when the bot status/config endpoint is loaded.

### 3. Keep live entry disabled

Your actual `server/.env` should remain:

```env
TRADING_MODE=paper
ENABLE_LIVE_TRADING=false
```

### 4. Reset and run virtual money

Reset the paper wallet to something like:

```text
$10,000 virtual balance
```

Then start only the **Paper TradingBot**.

Monitor:

- active strategy;
- rejected setup reasons;
- regime;
- setup type;
- virtual positions;
- realized P&L;
- profit factor;
- drawdown;
- losing streak;
- fees and slippage.

## Checks completed

- Server safety tests: **7 passed**
- Strategy and integration tests: **8 passed**
- Modified server JavaScript syntax checks: passed
- Client production build: passed
- Client lint still has existing issues and is not clean

The strategy is now a real new trading pipeline, not a renamed old one. But the initial test result is mixed, so it should be treated as a paper research candidate—not promoted to live trading yet.

## Assistant (Build · GPT 6 Astra · 37.7s)

_Thinking:_



That message is the bot’s **daily-loss circuit breaker**, not a market-signal failure.

The exact calculation is:

```text
daily loss so far:       $0.5788
daily loss limit:        $0.5000
=> new entries blocked
```

The bot checks this gate **before** it analyzes the market. That is why RSI, ATR, volume, spread, and other fields show `—`, and why the regime appears `UNCERTAIN`: no market scan was performed after the account-risk gate stopped the cycle.

There are two likely sources of the confusing `$100` display:

1. The UI currently labels `walletBalance` as **“Wallet / Budget”**, but that value is only the available wallet balance.
2. The daily limit uses:

```js
effectiveBudget = min(config.budgetUSDT, availableBalance)
maxDailyLoss = effectiveBudget * maxDailyLossPct / 100
```

So if your wallet is `$100`, but the bot’s stored strategy budget is `$10` and daily loss is `5%`, the limit is:

```text
$10 × 5% = $0.50
```

That exactly matches your message. The `$100` shown in the audit card is probably wallet balance, not the configured strategy budget.

Also, the `$0.5788` is the sum of today’s closed **net losses**, including fees and slippage. It is not necessarily one trade. The current daily reset is based on **UTC midnight**, not local India time.

I’m correcting the misleading UI label now so it separately shows:

- Available wallet
- Effective strategy budget

The intended behavior is: once the daily loss limit is reached, the bot stops opening new paper positions until the next UTC day. It does not mean the data feed is broken.

## Assistant (Build · GPT 6 Astra · 27.7s)

This is happening because the bot’s **daily-loss safety limit has been reached**.

```text
Daily loss so far: $0.5788
Daily loss limit:  $0.5000
```

Since:

```text
$0.5788 > $0.5000
```

the bot correctly blocks new entries.

## Why the market indicators show `—`

The safety gate runs **before** market analysis. Once the account-risk check fails, the bot does not scan RSI, ATR, volume, spread, or candles.

Therefore these are expected:

```text
5m RSI:       —
1m RSI:       —
5m ATR:       —
Volume Ratio: —
Spread:       —
Regime:       UNCERTAIN
```

The bot is not saying the market is uncertain. It is saying:

> “I did not analyze the market because account risk already blocked trading.”

## Why the limit is only `$0.50`

The UI previously labeled the wallet balance as **“Wallet / Budget”**, but it was only showing wallet balance.

The actual calculation is:

```js
effectiveBudget = min(strategyBudget, availableWallet)
dailyLossLimit = effectiveBudget × maxDailyLossPct / 100
```

For example, if your configuration is:

```text
Available wallet: $100
Strategy budget:  $10
Maximum daily loss: 5%
```

then:

```text
$10 × 5% = $0.50
```

That matches your displayed limit.

So your `$100` is probably your available virtual wallet, while the bot’s strategy budget is still `$10`.

## What I changed in the UI

The audit panel now separately displays:

- `Available Wallet`
- `Effective Budget`

I also changed the confusing badge from:

```text
SAFETY COOLDOWN
```

to:

```text
SAFETY GATE
```

because this condition can represent a daily-loss limit, consecutive-loss limit, or temporary cooldown.

The client production build passed after this change.

## What you should do

Open TradingBot settings and decide which budget you actually want.

For a `$100` virtual wallet, a reasonable paper configuration is:

```text
Strategy budget:       $100
Risk per trade:        1%
Maximum daily loss:    5%
Maximum leverage:      5x
Maximum open positions: 1
```

That would produce:

```text
Daily loss limit = $100 × 5% = $5
```

Your current loss of `$0.5788` would then be below the daily limit.

Alternatively, keep the `$10` strategy budget. Then the `$0.50` daily stop is intentional and quite conservative.

## Important

Resetting the paper wallet does not necessarily erase the bot’s previous `BotTrade` loss records. The daily-loss calculation is based on today’s closed bot trades, using UTC date boundaries.

The bot will resume automatically after the daily period resets, or after you save a larger effective strategy budget. Do not delete trade history just to bypass the safety gate; the loss should remain visible in performance statistics.
